Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The math_analysis operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 234. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 682 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the math_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 234 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 682. QED.
